# Pipeline Coordination Ledger

**Purpose:** Single source of truth for parallel pipeline controllers (this session, scheduled ticks, human dispatchers). Read this file from a fresh clone BEFORE dispatching, green-lighting, or deploying. A green-light contradicting an ACTIVE HOLD must name the hold and state why it is released.

**Stamp doctrine:** Re-read the sandbox clock (`date -u`) immediately before writing any timestamp — including this ledger's "Last updated" field and any function BUILD_STAMP. Never carry a stamp forward from an earlier turn.

**Backend access law (CEO standing order, 2026-07-24):** ALL backend/database access for this project goes through Lovable `query_database` (project 75bce9a1-c7dc-4628-aea5-12baa2e26bf2) — no exceptions. The Supabase MCP connector pointing at project `viqqhoxtakscdiazhvwf` is a DEAD project: INACTIVE is its expected and permanent state. Never query, restore, pause, modify, or "fix" it, and never treat its INACTIVE status as an incident. Any controller session (tick, interactive, or fresh reset) that attempts direct Supabase access is misbehaving per the mutex rule.

**Leak-prevention phases apply to ALL products (CEO order 2026-07-25):** every product generator must adopt Phase 0 (customer-message catalog + FIELD_LABELS for its intake fields), Phase 1 (emit-gate wired pre-write), and Phase 2 (report schema + whitelist serializer) in its next T2 product-update turn; Phase 3 rides the next major turn thereafter. No product turn may be marked DONE without P0-P2 adoption or an explicit UNCORRECTABLE-style deviation ruling. Full scope in §8.

## 3. Deploy Locks

**Standing rule:** Deploying any edge function requires:
- (a) No in-flight customer-path generation on that function — query the relevant product table for rows created in the last 15 minutes with a NULL report/document payload.
- (b) No `quality_batch_runs` row in `running` or `pending` status that dispatches the function.

If either check returns a row, the deploy WAITS until the run reaches a terminal state (`complete`, `error`, `cancelled`).

- **Turn:** `DS-T2b-ORCH-WIRING` — team-reviewed (five-lens); deploy on `quality-batch-orchestrator` + `delivery-sentinel`; cron migration applied.
- **Real-time:** 2026-07-25T01:48:37Z (orchestrator boot log; sentinel deployed and sweeping every minute; sandbox `date -u` at lock re-check 01:47:25Z; BUILD_STAMP fresh-clock 01:47:25Z).
- **Scope (three-front change, no customer-surface impact):** (1) wire `quality-batch-orchestrator` to the DS-T1 `delivery_contracts` helper — create at kickoff, heartbeat on existing cadence, terminate on terminal transitions, all fail-open; (2) schedule `delivery-sentinel` every 60s via pg_cron (idempotent unschedule-then-schedule); (3) extend the sentinel harness branch to auto-reconcile `quality_batch_runs` subjects on `harness_stalled`.
- **Root cause (from wave-13 incident + §7 log):** THIRD orchestrator isolate death in one day (waves 10, 11, 13). `quality_batch_runs` were not registered as `delivery_contracts`, so the DS-T2 sweep could not catch orchestrator death; recovery required manual SQL to flip the batch row. Root fix is to make the orchestrator itself a first-class delivery-contract subject and let the sentinel reconcile.
- **Fixes shipped this turn:**
  1. **New `supabase/functions/quality-batch-orchestrator/_contract_hooks.ts`** (`ContractDeps`, `dcCreateBatchContract`, `dcHeartbeatBatchContract`, `dcTerminateBatchContract`): all three helpers wrap the DS-T1 helper in try/catch and log-then-swallow. Contract failure NEVER alters batch behavior. `dcCreateBatchContract` always sets `runClass="harness"`, `tool="quality-batch"`, `subjectTable="quality_batch_runs"`, `userId=null`. `dcTerminateBatchContract` maps orchestrator status → terminal state: `complete → delivered`; `cancelled → harness_stalled`; `failed`/`error → admin_escalated`.
  2. **Wiring (`quality-batch-orchestrator/index.ts`):** BUILD_STAMP `ds-t2b-orch-wiring@2026-07-25T01:47:25Z`. Subject-keyed `CONTRACT_DEPS` looks up the live contract by `(subject_table, subject_id, terminal_state IS NULL)` for heartbeat/terminate. Injection points: `startRun` (single/free-form batch), `startPinnedRerunBatch`, `startCampaignWave` — each creates a contract with a `checkpointRef` carrying batch/campaign metadata. `heartbeat(runId)` calls `dcHeartbeatBatchContract` on the existing cadence — no new timers. `markTerminalAll` maps `patch.status` → terminal state. Outer isolate-death `catch` in the `Deno.serve` handler also calls `dcTerminateBatchContract("failed", err)` so isolate crashes still terminate the contract cleanly.
  3. **Sentinel harness reconciliation (`delivery-sentinel/index.ts`):** new exported `reconcileQualityBatchRun(admin, subjectId, note)` writes `status=cancelled, phase=done, last_error="[delivery-sentinel] <note>", completed_at=now()` to `quality_batch_runs` with `.not("status","in","(complete,failed,cancelled)")` so terminal rows are untouched. Called from `handleHarness` after `terminate(..., "harness_stalled", ...)` when `subject_table === "quality_batch_runs"`. Fail-open; contract termination stands regardless. Build stamp bumped to `ds-t2b@2026-07-25T01:47:25Z` in both the header comment and the response JSON.
  4. **Cron migration (`delivery-sentinel-sweep`):** idempotent `DO $$ IF EXISTS ... PERFORM cron.unschedule(...) $$` then `cron.schedule('delivery-sentinel-sweep', '* * * * *', ...)` using `net.http_post` with `Content-Type`, `x-internal-cron: 1`, and `Authorization: Bearer` sourced from `vault.decrypted_secrets` (`ADMIN_SECRET_TOKEN`). Job id 103. Sentinel was already firing pre-migration; migration standardizes the schedule.
  5. **`supabase/config.toml`:** added `[functions.delivery-sentinel] verify_jwt = false` so the cron POST reaches the function without a signed JWT (`x-internal-cron` + service-role bearer are the auth surface).
- **Deploy discipline:**
  - Pre-deploy lock re-check at 01:47:25Z (sandbox `date -u`): `quality_batch_runs` running/pending = 0; `cppa_assessments` <15 min NULL report_data = 0 (all modules). Both green.
  - Deploy landed 01:48:02Z, ~17 min ahead of the 02:05Z freeze / ~27 min ahead of the 02:15Z wave-17 launch. Orchestrator is the wave harness, so this deploy would have been forbidden mid-wave; the dispatch's ACTIVE HOLD contingency was not triggered.
  - BUILD_STAMPs fresh-clock from `date -u` immediately before write: `ds-t2b-orch-wiring@2026-07-25T01:47:25Z` (orchestrator) + `ds-t2b@2026-07-25T01:47:25Z` (sentinel).
  - Post-deploy boot proof: orchestrator log 01:48:37Z shows `[qb-orchestrator] boot ds-t2b-orch-wiring@2026-07-25T01:47:25Z`. Sentinel sweeps every minute (01:44:01Z→01:48:02Z, `{"evt":"delivery_sentinel_sweep","scanned":0,"duration_ms":<100–260>}`).

## 7. Incident Log

### 2026-07-24 — Split-Brain Green-Light on ADMT-FIX-W9
- **Timeline:** Digest-analysis controller placed ADMT-FIX-W9 on HOLD (plan-only, pending diagnosis). A sibling controller filed and independently green-lit the fix on its own gate. Deploy of `run-admt-checker` landed ~11:49Z coincident with in-flight customer generation `8f6b316d` (invoked 11:47Z).
- **Outcome (fortunate):** All three in-flight sample generations reached `complete` with valid reports. No isolate death observed on the customer path.
- **Root cause:** Controllers shared no readable state; HOLD was not durable outside its originator's session. Sibling turn also skipped the re-read-sandbox-clock-before-stamping practice — third occurrence — producing a future-dated `@…T12:30:00Z` build marker.
- **Remediation:** This ledger. Standing header rule (read before dispatch). Deploy Locks §3 include the in-flight customer-run check, not batches only. Stamp doctrine reasserted in header and applied to §4 timestamps.

### 2026-07-24 — Wave-10 Orchestrator Stall (batch `2ec63cd3`)
- Isolate died 11:15:27Z after `cppa-risk` termination; `dpia`+`admt` completed independently; `cppa-cyber` never dispatched.
- **Reconciliation status: COMPLETE.** Row reads `status=cancelled`, `phase=done`, `completed_at=11:52:01Z`, `last_error` populated. `cancel_requested=true` preserved as a historical marker. No action outstanding.
- Recovery batch `5e0558f3` (see §5) now underway to reclaim the lost cppa-risk and cppa-cyber reads.
- DS-T2 sentinel would **not** have caught this (orchestrator runs not yet contracts).

### 2026-07-24 — Stale-Clone Queue Insertion (BATCH-5e0558f3-EXTRACT)
- **Timeline:** A tick controller drafted a queue insert from clone `e724d19` (12:07Z) and inserted `CPPA-CYBER-FIX-CN-PLACEHOLDER` as the next item. The insert was unaware that `RECOVERY-BATCH-FIXES / TURN A` — already green-lit and shipping the same placeholder fix plus deterministic aggregates — was deployed at 12:25Z. TURN B (`cppa-risk` field cross-wiring) was briefly demoted behind the duplicate item.
- **Outcome:** Duplicate work item removed; TURN B restored as NEXT within the same tick (~5 minutes). No code deployed or execution started for the duplicate.
- **Root cause:** Controller read the ledger at tick start and did not re-clone before dispatching an insert, while another parallel turn completed its work and updated the ledger in between.
- **Remediation:** Reinforces the standing header rule: **re-clone immediately before dispatch, not at tick start.** All controllers must read `docs/pipeline-state.md` from a fresh clone before acting.

### 2026-07-24 — Duplicate Courier Dispatch on SAMPLES-CONTRACT-lia
- A tick controller re-cloned at 12:50:10Z (HEAD `8b3fed9`, NEXT = `SAMPLES-CONTRACT-lia (5/8)`), spent ~4 minutes on fixture-drift analysis, and dispatched the 5/8 courier at ~12:54Z. A parallel controller had already completed 5/8 at 12:53:21Z (commit `d7efef9`) inside that window. The executing session made revised edits (duplicate) and committed them as `4fbf7e3b` — including a `potential_harm` change `"Severe"` → `"Moderate"` and additions of `stage`/`status`/`preview_signal` to `ALLOWED_TOPLEVEL_EXTRAS`. Supersede notice arrived ~13:00Z; the duplicate edits were reverted to the `d7efef9` state (files `src/lib/sampleFixtures.ts` and `supabase/functions/_shared/intake-contracts/validate.ts` restored) at 13:03:07Z. The landed rubric value `"Severe"` stands as the turn of record.
- Root cause: final re-clone happened before analysis/composition, not immediately before send. The stale-clone remediation ("re-clone immediately before dispatch") must mean: compose first, then fresh-clone verify the queue item is still open, then send within the same minute.
- Remediation (standing): controllers compose the courier message BEFORE the final clone check; the final check verifies ledger "Last updated" stamp and queue state; any stamp movement aborts the send.

- **Standing remediation (reasserted):** compose the courier → **final** fresh `docs/pipeline-state.md` ledger check (verify "Last updated" stamp AND that the target queue item is still open) → send within the same minute; any stamp movement between the final check and send aborts the send.

### 2026-07-25 — WAVE-18 SENTINEL FALSE-KILL (batch `cf953d1c`, contract `366a6028`)
- **Timeline:** Wave-18 batch `cf953d1c-276b-4b14-9db5-5a237fecc526` (tools cppa-admt/cppa-risk/cppa-cyber; batch_size 3; campaign `fd1be147`) launched 04:45:02Z. Paired delivery contract `366a6028-eeaa-4037-a3df-9316f973ecff` hit terminal `harness_stalled` at 04:48:01Z (`stage=generate attempts=4`) and `reconcileQualityBatchRun` flipped the batch to `cancelled` — while all three per-tool `quality_runs` (`447d76f8` admt, `376bdbfe` risk, `230e2609` cyber) were ALIVE and heartbeating (last_heartbeat 04:49:2xZ; cyber already on doc index 1).
- **Root cause (three-part):** (1) HARNESS_SLA `stageSeconds:180 / overallSeconds:900` was structurally too tight for a real 3-tool × 3-doc wave (~35–40 min), so stage + overall deadlines were guaranteed to breach every wave. (2) Sentinel `handleHarness` stageStale predicate was inverted (`heartbeat_at < stage_deadline_at` is TRUE for every freshly-created contract with a future deadline), so every 60s sweep bumped attempts even on brand-new contracts; three sweeps → `attempts>=3` → forced harness_stalled + reconcile-to-cancelled with no liveness cross-check. (3) Orchestrator `heartbeatBySubject` swallowed lookup / write errors silently, so wave-18's contract heartbeat never landed and no evidence was left (wave-17's DID land, which is the only reason wave-17 survived: once `heartbeat_at > stage_deadline_at` the inverted `stageStale` predicate went permanently false).
- **DS-T2c fix (deployed 2026-07-25T04:55:31Z):** HARNESS_SLA bumped to `900/5400` (`_shared/delivery-contract.ts` `ds-t2c@2026-07-25T04:53:00Z`); sentinel harness branch gains LIVENESS GUARD (`delivery-sentinel/index.ts` `ds-t2c-sentinel-livenessguard@2026-07-25T04:53:30Z`) that reads batch AND in-flight child quality_run heartbeats and refreshes the contract (no attempt bump) when either is fresher than 5 min; stageStale predicate corrected on BOTH branches; orchestrator (`quality-batch-orchestrator/index.ts` `ds-t2c-orch-hbfix@2026-07-25T04:54:00Z`) emits structured heartbeat-failure events. All contract calls still fail-open.
- **Wave-18 reconcile:** `quality_batch_runs cf953d1c` restored to `status=running/phase=running_tool` (last_error cleared, completed_at nulled, cancel_requested false); fresh contract `d3836dd5` minted under the new 900/5400 SLA (`checkpoint_ref.origin=ds-t2c-hotfix-reconcile`, `supersedes_contract=366a6028`); historic terminal `366a6028` preserved untouched. Orchestrator resume invoked via pg_net; batch re-adopted (last_heartbeat 04:56:22Z; contract heartbeat 04:56:22Z confirms the piggyback fix works). No tool-run restart / duplicate; the three in-flight quality_runs continue to completion under the new SLA.
- **Remediation:** DS-T2c ships all three fixes atomically. Colocated deno **19/19 PASS** (7/7 SLA + 3/3 liveness + 3/3 reconcile + 6/6 contract hooks). See §2 item 20 for full detail.

### 2026-07-25 — Wave-18 Orchestrator Isolate Death #4 + DS-T2c Sentinel Liveness-Guard Gap
- **Timeline:** Wave-18 batch `cf953d1c` orchestrator heartbeat stopped inside the DS-T2c-protected window; batch remained `running/pending` with all three child `quality_runs` (`447d76f8` admt / `376bdbfe` risk / `230e2609` cyber) reaching TERMINAL state independently and per-run digests written. Row sat un-reaped for 40+ min despite child terminality; reconciled at 05:49:08Z by the interactive controller (`status=cancelled`, `phase=done`, `last_error` populated) per wave-10/11/13/18-false-kill precedent. NO reads lost.
- **Fourth orchestrator isolate death in one day** — pattern persists after DS-T2c-ORCH-HBFIX (04:55:31Z). Structured `dc_heartbeat_*` events (added in orchestrator ds-t2c-orch-hbfix) will help diagnose future occurrences.
- **New finding — DS-T2c sentinel liveness-guard gap:** the LIVENESS GUARD refreshes the contract (no attempt bump) whenever batch OR child heartbeats are fresh — but does NOT reap a batch whose child `quality_runs` are ALL terminal for ≥ N minutes and whose own heartbeat is stale. Correct exit condition for the harness branch: "if all subject_ref children are terminal AND parent batch heartbeat is stale AND stage deadline breached, reconcile-to-cancelled" (do NOT wait for HARNESS_SLA overall deadline to fire — it never fires when nothing is heartbeating).
- **Follow-up queued:** `DS-T2d-SENTINEL-TERMINAL-CHILDREN-REAP` — extend `handleHarness` liveness guard with an "all-children-terminal" branch that reconciles the batch immediately when: (a) every child quality_run is `complete`/`error`/`cancelled`; (b) parent batch heartbeat is stale > 5 min; (c) contract stageDeadline breached. Fail-open. Colocated deno test with fixture reproducing wave-18 shape. Sequenced after items 28-31; pre-empts DS-T2c gap.
